- "Save draft" keeps the marks as typed (blank or out of range is allowed in a draft). It lives in a new store `approvalOfResults:drafts` (`DRAFTS_KEY` in `api.ts`), one draft per sitting with no batch, and is removed when the batch is submitted. A draft is refused once a batch exists. "Submit results" stays disabled until every row passes; the api checks again. The QP's Awaiting Submission list shows a "Draft saved" badge and the button reads "Continue".
- Narrow screens: the table scrolls sideways inside its card (the learner column stays in view); the page does not.

### File 3 upload (same screen): done (9 Nov 2026)

- `file3.ts` + `external/File3Upload.tsx`. "Download template (CSV)": Learner ID number, Learner name, one column per component (header = component name), Absent (Y/N); one row per learner, marks blank.
- "Upload File 3 (CSV)" shows a preview with an error per row: unknown learner (valid ID, not on this batch), learner missing from the file, mark not a number, mark out of range, blank mark when not absent, Absent with marks, invalid ID number (the three shared messages), the same learner twice, and an Absent value that is not Y or N. A missing component (or Absent / ID) column rejects the whole file with a clear message; extra columns are ignored.
- A clean file fills the grid and the QP can still edit; a file with errors does not change the grid. CSV only (a file with semicolons also reads, as in the Calendar upload).

### 05.23 — One South African ID number check: done (9 Nov 2026)

- `shared/validation/saIdNumber.ts`: `saIdNumberIssue()` / `isValidSaIdNumber()`. 13 digits, the first six a real YYMMDD date, and a valid Luhn check digit. Messages: "ID number must be 13 digits", "The date of birth in the ID number is not a real date", "The ID number's check digit is wrong". The century is not in the number, so 29 February is accepted if either 19YY or 20YY is a leap year.
- Used in the File 3 upload and on the capture screen. A learner whose stored ID number is invalid is flagged under their name. It does not block Submit, because the QP cannot correct the learner record (the SDP / QA domain owns it).

- **Seed data fix:** the ID numbers of learner-001 to learner-012 had wrong check digits (learner-013 onwards were already valid). Only the last digit of those twelve was changed; names and the batches they are on are untouched.

### Assumptions (docs only, not on screen)

- **File 3 columns are our own simple version** until QCTO confirms its File 3 format (open question below).
- Learners in the file are matched on ID number only; the name column is for the QP's reading and is not checked.
- Absent column: Y / Yes marks absent; N / No or blank means present. Marks may use a decimal comma.
- A flagged ID number on the capture screen is a warning, not a block (see 05.23).
- The batch figures strip counts the rows that are complete so far (so a half-typed grid does not count blanks as "not yet competent"); a note says how many rows it is based on. Once every row is complete it is the whole batch.
- Evidence documents are not part of a draft (file names only, UI-only as before).

## Batch L2.1 (CSV files that work with Excel on South African settings, learner ID numbers on submitted batches)

Found in testing: Excel in South Africa uses ";" as the list separator and "," as the decimal mark, so comma templates opened as one column, re-saved files came back with semicolons, and IDs starting with 0 lost the 0.

- **Shared CSV code** (`shared/csv/csv.ts`): `withExcelPrefix()` puts a UTF-8 BOM and a `sep=,` line on every downloaded template (File 3 and the EISA Calendar dates template). `parseCsvTable()` skips a `sep=` first line and tries the delimiter it names first, otherwise picks `,` or `;` by which one splits the heading row into the expected columns. Headings match ignoring case, extra spaces and a BOM. If neither works the file is rejected with the Excel message ("Excel may have changed the file's format. Download the template again…"), which now also covers a genuinely missing column (the old "missing the required column" text is gone). Used by `approval-of-results/file3.ts` and `eisa-calendar/bulkUpload.ts`.
- **Leading 0** (`shared/validation/saIdNumber.ts`, `withRestoredLeadingZero()`): a 12-digit ID is stored with a 0 in front only when the 13-digit result is a valid SA ID number. The File 3 preview shows "Leading 0 restored (Excel removes it)" on that row. Any other 12-digit ID still says "ID number must be 13 digits". File 3 is the only learner upload today; later uploads should call the same helper.
- **Decimal comma**: "45,5" is read as 45.5 in a semicolon file and when quoted in a comma file (the number rule already accepted it; now covered by checks).
- **Help line** under the File 3 and Calendar upload controls (`EXCEL_UPLOAD_TIP`).
- **Calendar dates**: 2028/03/15, 15/03/2028 and 2028-03-15 still work after the delimiter change.
- **Learner ID numbers on batches**: `EisaLearnerResult.idNumber` and FISA `LearnerResult.idNumber` are new, stored when the batch is submitted (`submitResultsBatch` takes them from the learner record, so capture and File 3 both end up with them). Shown next to the name in `EisaLearnerResultsTable` (QP batch detail, internal Batch Results and the learner detail), in `FisaLearnerResultsList` (QP and internal) and in the SDP's FISA learner picker, and as an "ID number" column in both verification report downloads. The seeds fill them from `ENROLLED_LEARNERS`.
- **SEED_VERSION is now 'A13.1'** (results stored before this had no ID numbers, so local demo data is rebuilt once).
- **Assumptions**: the Excel message replaces the missing-column message; IDs are not written as text in the template (Excel may still drop the 0 on edit, which the restore handles); a `sep=` delimiter that fails falls back to detection.

**Checked:** 7-weekday seed check (14 runs, all pass) with new cases: comma, semicolon, `sep=,` and `sep=;` files; loose header case/spaces/BOM; the mixed header rejected with the new message; 12-digit ID restored; an invalid 12-digit ID still rejected; "45,5" in a semicolon file and quoted in a comma file; Calendar semicolon file with 15/03/2028, 2028/03/17 and 2028-03-20; both templates start with BOM and `sep=,` and read back; every seeded EISA batch, draft and FISA batch has a valid ID number per learner; a newly submitted batch stores one per learner; both verification reports list them. eslint and the scoped tsc check pass clean.

## Batch L1.1 (QCTO's QAS Addendum Control Sheet and EISA Calculator)

QCTO sent two documents on 9 Nov 2026: the "QAS Addendum Control Sheet" (what QCTO sends to Certification, one row per component) and the "EISA Calculator (updated 01/12/2021)". The QASA assessment details and the EISA results calculation now follow them. Builds on K, L1, L2 and L2.1. EISA only; FISA results are unchanged (QCTO confirmed FISA has no calculation).

### QASA Assessment Details = the control sheet: done (9 Nov 2026)

- **Per component** (`qsa-addendum/types.ts`, `assessmentDetails.ts`, `components/AssessmentDetailsEditor.tsx`): component no (the order), name, type and days (kept, QCTO asked for them separately), weighting %, "Highest mark (component total)" and "Lowest mark (competence mark for this component)". `totalMarks` / `passMark` are renamed `highestMark` / `lowestMark`; records stored before this batch are mapped by `normaliseComponent()` (totalMarks → highest, passMark → lowest).
- **Rules** (`validateAssessmentDetails`): highest mark a whole number of at least 1; lowest mark a whole number from 0 to the highest; weighting a whole number from 1 to 100 ("Each Total needs a valid Weighting"); weightings add up to exactly 100 ("Total weighted percentages must be exactly 100"); at most 5 components, the calculator's limit (the Add button stops at 5 and the message says why).
- **Per QASA:** "Addendum pass %" (whole number 1 to 100) and "FLC required" (Yes/No), both required once any component is listed. "Addendum start date" is set automatically to the day the CEO signs the feedback letter (stored in the Certification hand-off). "Addendum end date" is the qualification registration end date from Batch K. Number of components and Total marks (the sum of the highest marks) are derived and shown, not typed.
- **Validity line** (`qasaValidityText`) is now "Valid for the lifespan of the qualification: <start> to <end>" (this also feeds the feedback letter text). Before the CEO signs, the start reads "set when the CEO signs the feedback letter".
- **Shown in** the ASD editor, the Details card (internal and QP, `AssessmentDetailsCard.tsx`) and the "Certification notified of the assessment details" snapshot (the hand-off now also holds the pass %, FLC and both dates).
- **Download control sheet (CSV)** (`qsa-addendum/controlSheet.ts`): a button on the internal Details card of an approved QASA. One row per component with exactly QCTO's 14 headings in QCTO's order and spelling (including "Componenet no"): AQP, Qualification Title, Qualification ID, NQF Level, Number of components, Total Marks, Addendum Pass %, Addendum Start Date, Addendum End Date, Componenet no, Lowest Mark, Highest Mark, Weighting, FLC. Dates are YYYY-MM-DD, FLC is Y/N, the file starts with the BOM and `sep=,` (shared writer). It uses the approved values (the hand-off), so a review in progress does not change it.

### EISA results calculation = QCTO's calculator: done (9 Nov 2026)

- **`approval-of-results/calculation.ts`** (header cites "QCTO EISA Calculator, updated 01/12/2021"): component percentage = mark ÷ highest mark × 100; weighted value = component percentage × weighting ÷ 100; EISA Percentage Obtained (decimal) = the sum of the weighted values rounded to 2 decimals; EISA Percentage Obtained (final) = that rounded half-up to a whole number. Per component: Competent when the mark is at least the lowest mark. Learner: Competent when the final percentage is at least the Addendum pass % AND competent in every component; otherwise Not yet competent with the reason "Below pass %" and/or "Not yet competent in <component>". This replaces the L1 rule. Batch figures are unchanged (registered, wrote, absent, competent, not yet competent, competency rate = competent ÷ wrote, absenteeism = absent ÷ registered) and the 45% Post-EISA rule is unchanged.
- **Whole-number marks** (`markEntry.ts`): "Marks must be whole numbers" on the capture screen, the File 3 checks and `submitResultsBatch`. The decimal-comma reading from L2.1 stays in the File 3 parser, but such a mark is now rejected with that message.
- **Snapshot** (`componentSnapshot.ts`): a batch copies the Addendum pass % (`ResultsBatch.addendumPassPercent`) and each component's lowest and highest mark, so later QASA changes do not move an existing batch. A QASA with components but no pass % blocks capture with its own message.
- **On screen:** capture screen (a mark input per component with its component % and C/NYC under it, then EISA % decimal / final, pass % and the result with its reason), `EisaLearnerResultsTable` for the QP batch detail and the internal Batch Results (mark = "EISA Component Achievement Value", component %, C/NYC; the learner detail adds the weighted value), and the verification report (columns for decimal and final EISA Percentage Obtained, pass % and the result with its reason; the batch details list the Addendum pass %).

### Seed (`SEED_VERSION = 'A13.2'`)

- Every QASA with components has an Addendum pass % and FLC: Electrical Trades 50 / No, Welding 60 / Yes, Plumbing 55 / No, Gas Practitioner 70 / Yes, Millwright 55 / No; the highest and lowest marks and weightings are as before (weightings add up to 100).
- The marks of the 10 EISA batches are rebuilt as whole numbers. Every batch keeps its stage and its competent / not yet competent pattern and rate (Welding 40% below 45% with its Post-EISA visit, Electrical Trades 50% with none, the others as in L1). Not yet competent learners at an even position pass overall but are not yet competent in one component (8 such learners in the seed); 3 learners are also below the pass %. The L2 draft and the empty awaiting batch are valid with whole numbers.

### Assumptions (docs only, not on screen)

- The control sheet "AQP" is the QP's name, the NQF Level is the number only, and FLC is a Yes/No answer (see Open questions).
- An approved QASA stored before this batch has no pass % or FLC until the ASD edits it; the control sheet leaves those cells blank and a results batch for it is blocked until the pass % is added.
- File 3's layout is unchanged (open question about one row per learner per EISA date).

**Checked:** 7-weekday seed check (Mon 2 to Sun 8 Nov 2026, 00:30 and 23:30 UTC, 14 runs, all pass): 13 QASAs, 5 with components all valid with a pass % (50, 55, 60, 70) and FLC both Yes and No, the 4 hand-offs holding pass %, FLC, start (the signing day) and end (the registry date), Millwright with no start date yet; 10 batches at the same stages and the same rates, Welding 40% with its Post-EISA visit, Electrical 50% with none; every mark a whole number, every batch's pass % equal to its QASA's; QCTO's test case (totals 90 and 555, weightings 50 and 50, marks 78 and 0 → 43.33, final 43), 44.5 → 45, 44.49 → 44, 1.4 → 1 and 1.5 → 2; a learner who passes overall but is not yet competent in one component (65% against a pass % of 50, "Not yet competent in Knowledge"), both reasons together, a mark exactly at the lowest mark is competent; 45.5 refused by the entry check, by File 3 (semicolon file with "45,5") and by the api, a whole-number File 3 accepted; the control sheet download starts with the BOM and `sep=,`, has QCTO's 14 headings exactly, one row per component with the right values (Welding 250 total marks, pass % 60, FLC Y); validation: 6 components, pass % 0 / 101 / 50.5 / missing, FLC missing, weightings 60+30, lowest above highest, highest 0, decimal highest and decimal weighting all refused, 5 components and lowest 0 accepted, an empty list accepted; old totalMarks / passMark records map correctly; through the api: saving without a pass % is refused, saving with it works, removing all components clears it, a submitted batch snapshots the pass % and each component's lowest and highest mark; the verification report has the new columns and the pass %. `npx eslint src/features/assessments-v2` and the scoped `tsc -p` check (temporary `tsconfig.check.json`, deleted afterwards) pass clean. Not yet clicked through in a browser.

## Batch M (QCTO change request doc v14 — EISA results submission documents)

Rows 29.23, 29.24, 29.25, 29.26 and 05.17. Builds on L1, L1.1, L2 and L2.1. `SEED_VERSION = 'A14'`.

### Done (9 Nov 2026)

- **29.23 — tagged EISA evidence** (`approval-of-results/external/EisaEvidenceUpload.tsx`, `config.ts`): a tag from a fixed list (Marking report, Moderation report, Confirmation letter, Attendance register, Other evidence), one instruction line above the upload area, the same pick-a-tag-then-file pattern as FISA. Submit is blocked until there is a Marking report and a Moderation report, and the blocking list names what is missing (`eisaSubmissionIssues` in `workflow.ts`, enforced again in `api.ts`). Tags show on the internal Details and Compliance Review tabs, on the QP's batch detail and in the verification report download. The ASD's own review upload is tagged Other evidence. FISA tags are unchanged.
- **29.25 — "Already on file"** (`external/OnFileDocumentsSection.tsx`, `mock/data/onFileDocuments.ts`): six documents (confidentiality agreement for the instrument developer and for the moderator, CV of each, instrument development report, instrument validation report). Each has Confirm or Replace; Submit is blocked until all six are decided. Each choice is stored on the batch (`onFileDocuments`), written to the audit trail (`onFileAuditText`) and listed with the choice on the internal workspace, the QP's detail and the verification report.
- **29.26 — declaration**: "I confirm that the results and evidence submitted are accurate and complete." Submit stays disabled until ticked. Stored as `declaration` (same shape as the FISA one); the internal workspace shows "Declaration made by <name> on <date>"; also in the audit trail and the verification report.
- **29.24 — empty files** (`shared/files/fileCheck.ts`, `FileCheckNotice.tsx`): one helper. A 0-byte file shows "The file is empty." and is not added; a good file shows "Document checked". Covers EISA evidence, Replace on "Already on file", File 3, FISA evidence (SDP draft), the ASD's review uploads (EISA and FISA) and the EISA Calendar postponement/cancellation letters. The api functions refuse a 0-byte file too. The Calendar's dates CSV upload (not a document) is unchanged.
- **05.17 — "Documents for this batch"** (`batchDocuments.ts`, `components/BatchDocumentsCard.tsx`): on the internal workspace and the external batch detail, for both tabs. EISA: Outcome letter, Assessment Assurance Verification Report (EISA), Tracking register (audit trail), File 3 (learner results), Certification recommendation letter, Learner EISA submission format. FISA: Outcome letter, FISA Assessment Assurance Verification Report, Learner enrolment spreadsheet (skills programme), SP certification recommendation letter, Data load specification (skills programme). No EISA document is in the FISA set. The outcome letter links to the existing approval letter download and the verification report to the existing report, once the batch is approved. **Hooks for the next batch:** build the outcome letter, recommendation letters and audit-trail download, then add their download function against the document id in the `downloads` map where the card is rendered (`eisaDownloads()` / `fisaDownloads()` in the two internal workspaces, and the external details). Anything without a download shows "Generated at final approval".

### Seed

Every submitted EISA batch has a Marking report and a Moderation report (some also an attendance register or confirmation letter), the declaration by the QP at submission, and all six "Already on file" documents decided — one replaced per batch. Audit trail: six choices and the declaration per batch. Stages, rates, marks and the Welding Post-EISA visit are as in L1.1; the Electrical Trades (empty) and Welding (draft) sittings still await submission.

### Assumptions

- The EISA tag list and the "Already on file" list are our reading of the 29 Oct meeting until QCTO confirms them. Both are constants (`EISA_EVIDENCE_TAG_OPTIONS`, `ON_FILE_DOCUMENT_LABELS`).
- Marking report and Moderation report are the two required tags (`EISA_REQUIRED_EVIDENCE_TAGS`).
- The validated EISA instrument record only keeps the instrument file, so none of the six documents exist there yet. They come from clearly mock entries in `mock/data/onFileDocuments.ts`, the one place to change when the validation record holds them.
- Only empty files are refused. **Full content checks (AI or rules) are for the build, and QCTO still has to say what makes each document correct.**
- The "Documents for this batch" card shows downloads only once a batch is approved, as the existing download buttons do.

**Checked:** 7-weekday seed check (Mon 2 to Sun 8 Nov 2026, 00:30 and 23:30 UTC, 14 runs, all pass): 10 EISA batches at the same stages and rates (Welding 40% with its one Post-EISA visit); every batch has tagged non-empty evidence with a marking and a moderation report, a declaration by its QP, six decided "Already on file" documents (one replaced) and the matching audit entries; two sittings still awaiting (Welding with its draft, Electrical empty). Through the api: submit refused without the marking report, without the moderation report, without the declaration, with an unconfirmed document, with "replaced" but no file, with a 0-byte evidence file and with a 0-byte replacement (nothing stored); a valid submit stores the declaration, six records and seven audit entries; 0-byte uploads refused for the ASD's EISA and FISA review uploads, the FISA draft and the Calendar letter; the shared helper; no EISA document in the FISA set (names and ids); the report lists "Already on file". `npx eslint src/features/assessments-v2` and the scoped `tsc -p` check (temporary `tsconfig.check.json`, deleted afterwards) pass clean. Not yet clicked through in a browser.

## Batch M.1 (two bugs found testing Batch M)

No seed change (`SEED_VERSION` stays 'A14').

- **Evidence tags** (`external/EisaEvidenceUpload.tsx`, `config.ts`, `workflow.ts`): Marking report and Moderation report are single-use (`EISA_SINGLE_USE_EVIDENCE_TAGS`) and drop out of the picker once a file has the tag (`availableEisaEvidenceTags`); the other three stay. A file's tag is shown as text once added; to change it the QP removes the file and adds it again, which frees a single-use tag. `eisaSubmissionIssues` also refuses a repeated single-use tag, so the api enforces it too. The blocking list under Submit is worked out on every render, so it already updated at once; confirmed in the check. There is one EISA tagged upload (resubmission after a decline is a workflow action and does not re-edit evidence). FISA tags unchanged.
- **White modal on Replace** (`external/OnFileDocumentsSection.tsx`): the Replace control was a `<label>` around a visually hidden (`sr-only`, absolutely positioned) file input. That input was positioned against the modal, not its row, so when the browser focused it for the file picker it scrolled the modal's `overflow-hidden` shell and pushed the header and body out of view. Replace is now a button that opens a normal hidden file input. No nested dialog, no state change and no clearing of state was involved. The decision logic is `replaceOnFileDocument` in `workflow.ts`: an empty file changes nothing and returns "The file is empty."; a real file sets only that document's choice.
- **Error boundary** (`shared/ui/ModuleErrorBoundary.tsx`, `ModuleErrorFallback.tsx`, `index.tsx`): every module route and the dashboard are wrapped; a crash shows "Something went wrong on this screen. Your data is safe. Go back or reload." with a Back button and logs the error to the console. Keyed by route, so leaving the screen clears it.

**Checked:** 7-weekday seed check (14 runs, all pass) with new cases: all five tags offered at the start; a used Marking report drops out and the others stay; Confirmation letter, Attendance register and Other stay after repeated use; removing the file frees the tag; the blocking list changes as each report is added or removed; a repeated single-use tag is flagged and refused by the api; Replace with an empty file (message, choices untouched) then a real file, a second Replace, Replace after Confirm, an empty file after Confirm (stays confirmed), other documents' choices kept; a submit after those keeps all marks and files. `npx eslint src/features/assessments-v2` and the scoped `tsc -p` check (temporary `tsconfig.check.json`, deleted afterwards) pass clean. Not yet clicked through in a browser (the white-modal cause is from reading the code, not reproduced). `File3Upload.tsx` uses the same label + `sr-only` pattern; not changed here.

## Batch V1 (QCTO change request doc v14 — Site Visits: several qualifications per visit, "Approved with remediation")

`SEED_VERSION = 'A15'`. Rows 05.13 and 05.14 (QCTO's 5 Nov review).

### 05.13 — several qualifications on a Marking & Moderation or RPL/ARPL visit

- **Data** (`modules/site-visits/types.ts`, `visitQualifications.ts`): `SiteVisit.qualifications` is a list of `VisitQualification` (id, title, SAQA ID, sitting id, EISA date). `qualificationTitle` and `sittingId` still hold the first entry, so every list and module that reads one title keeps working. Only Marking & Moderation and RPL/ARPL carry a list (`isMultiQualificationVisit`); Post-EISA Monitoring and every other type keep their current fields.
- **Plan a New Visit** (`internal/NewVisitDialog.tsx`): add and remove rows. Marking & Moderation: each row picks a published EISA sitting, which fixes the qualification, its SAQA ID and its EISA date (the old optional single "Link to a published EISA sitting" is gone for this type). RPL/ARPL: each row picks a qualification (no sitting, item 2.7 holds). At least one row; a qualification can be added once (the picker disables it and `createVisit` refuses it). `createVisit` re-reads the published sittings, so title, SAQA ID and EISA date cannot disagree with the Calendar.
- **Shown on** the Details and Planning tabs (`components/QualificationList.tsx`), the All Visits list (first qualification + "and N more"), the QP's Visit Outcomes page, the Execution & Report tab, and the booking notification (`notificationText.ts`: "Qualifications to be checked: Electrical Trades (SAQA ID 91761) — EISA date 2026-…; …"). The seed builds the same text.
- **Script verification (3.7)**: each script row has a Qualification column; a visit with several qualifications refuses a row with none chosen. The sample size is shown per qualification and in total (`scriptSampleSizes`). A row stored before this batch belongs to the visit's only qualification.
- **Older records**: a stored visit with one qualification and one sitting is read as a one-item list by `visitQualificationsOf()` (nothing is rewritten in storage, so there is no migration to repeat). For such a Marking & Moderation visit the EISA date is the visit date, which is what the old form took from the linked sitting.
- **Approval of Results** finds a visit's Marking & Moderation report through any of its sittings (`visitSittingIdsOf`).

### 05.14 — "Approved (final)" and "Approved with remediation"

- **Workflow** (`workflow.ts`, `remediation.ts`, `config.ts`): the DD's single "Approve report" is now **Approved (final)** (same action id `report-approve`, so stored audit entries still read) and **Approved with remediation**. Two new stages: `remediation-awaiting` ("Approved with remediation — awaiting QP") and `remediation-review` ("Remediation submitted — DD review"). Stage `approved` is labelled "Approved (final)". Send back, re-route and reject are unchanged.
- **Approved with remediation**: the DD types the remediation items (required; the dialog button stays off and the api refuses blank items). The report and items are shared with the stakeholder automatically, overriding the share Yes/No default: for a visit with a QP `stakeholderShare` is set and the share card is locked ("Shared automatically because remediation was requested"; `setStakeholderShare` refuses to switch it off). The QP is notified.
- **QP side** (`external/VisitOutcomesPage.tsx`, `components/RemediationSection.tsx`): the visit appears on Visit Outcomes with its status, qualifications and the items. The QP adds evidence (button that clicks a plain hidden file input, the Batch M.1 pattern; empty files refused with `fileUploadIssue`, and `assertFileNotEmpty` in the api), types a response (required) and clicks **Submit remediation**. The DD is notified.
- **No QP** (RPL/ARPL at an SDP or assessment centre): there is nobody to share a QP view with and SDPs have no Site Visits view. The report and items are recorded as shared with the SDP / assessment centre (audit entry), the ASD is told to record the response, and **the ASD records the stakeholder's response and evidence on the visit** (Execution & Report tab) until a later batch gives SDPs their own view. The SDP also gets an in-app notification (as Post-EISA Monitoring already does). A special meeting has neither, so it can only be "Approved (final)".
- **DD review**: **Approved (final)** or **Further remediation needed** (items required, opens the next round and notifies the QP again).
- **History**: `SiteVisit.remediationRounds` keeps every round — items, who asked and when, who it was shared with, response, evidence (file names), who submitted (and whether the ASD recorded it) and the DD's decision. Shown on the visit and to the QP. Every step writes an audit entry: Approved with remediation (with the items), "Report and remediation items shared with …", each evidence file, Submit remediation (with the response), Further remediation needed (with the items), Approved (final).
- **Complete Visit** is only possible after Approved (final): the stage rule allows it only from `approved`, the api refuses it with a clear message in the other stages, and the workspace says so while remediation is open.
- **Dashboard** (`internal/InternalDashboard.tsx`): the two remediation stages count as reports received; a visit awaiting remediation is "my item" only for the ASD of a visit without a QP.

### Assumptions and open questions (Batch V1)

- **[OPEN — QCTO]** The status names ("Approved (final)", "Approved with remediation — awaiting QP", "Remediation submitted — DD review", "Further remediation needed") are ours. **QCTO still has to confirm them: Ms Rafir-Munsaur suggested a meeting to define the statuses.** They are constants in `modules/site-visits/config.ts`.
- **[OPEN — QCTO]** Until the SDP has its own Site Visits view, the ASD records an SDP's / assessment centre's remediation response. For such a visit the status reads "Approved with remediation — awaiting SDP" (or "awaiting assessment centre") instead of "awaiting QP".
- The QP directory has one qualification per QP, so a visit's qualification list is not restricted to the visit's QP (the Marking & Moderation row picks from every published sitting). The seeded three-qualification visit is Nomvula Dlamini's.
- A Marking & Moderation visit's date is entered by the planner; it is no longer filled from a sitting's date.
- A remediation response needs text; evidence is optional.
- Once remediation was requested the report stays shared with the QP (they have seen the items), so the share choice cannot be switched off.
- A QP is told by a role-level notification naming the QP (as every notification in this build).

### Seed (A15)

Four visits added after the original sixteen (which are unchanged): **SV17** Marking & Moderation with three qualifications (Electrical Trades, Welding, Plumbing — each with its own published EISA sitting, none shared with another visit) and a script verification of 5 scripts spread 2 / 2 / 1, report with the DD; **SV18** marking guideline discussion with Nomvula Dlamini, "Approved with remediation — awaiting QP" (she also has two earlier shared outcomes); **SV19** instrument validation visit with Precious Mahlangu, "Remediation submitted — DD review" after two rounds (round 1 answered and sent back for further remediation, round 2 with a response and two files); **SV20** RPL/ARPL at Khanya Skills Academy for Electrical Trades and Welding, report with the DD (use it to try the no-QP remediation path). The Welding Post-EISA visit is still "To be scheduled" and linked to its results batch.

**Checked:** 7-weekday seed check (Mon 2 to Sun 8 Nov 2026, 00:30 and 23:30 UTC, 14 runs, 98 checks each, all pass): 20 visits; the original 16 visits identical to the previous seed (compared field by field, ignoring only the new list and the script rows' qualification); a visit with three qualifications whose entries are different published sittings with their own EISA dates, and the booking notification listing all three with EISA dates (also when booking a new visit through the api); the RPL booking notification lists both qualifications with no EISA date; no duplicate qualification (two sittings of one qualification, and a repeated RPL qualification, are refused), none, and a Marking & Moderation row without a sitting are refused; sample sizes 2/2/1 (total 5) and 1/0/2 (total 3); a script row with no qualification flagged on a multi-qualification visit, and a stored row without one belongs to the only qualification; a stored one-qualification, one-sitting visit reads as a one-item list; remediation items required (none and blank); Complete Visit refused while awaiting the QP, while under DD review and passing after Approved (final); the QP sees the remediation (items, can respond) on Visit Outcomes with no share toggle used, and sharing cannot be switched off; empty evidence refused (QP and ASD), another QP and the ASD refused for a visit with a QP, a response required; the full loop approve with remediation → evidence → submit → further remediation (items required) → submit → Approved (final) → Complete Visit, with both rounds kept and every step in the audit trail; the QP notified on request, the DD on submission; the RPL path (shared with the SDP, ASD told, a QP refused, the ASD records the response and evidence, DD accepts); plain Approved (final) still works. `npx eslint src/features/assessments-v2` and the scoped `tsc -p` check (temporary `tsconfig.check.json`, deleted afterwards) pass clean. Not yet clicked through in a browser.

## Batch N1 (QCTO change request doc v14 — Outcome Letters, verification codes, final decline, Certification recommendation)

Rows 29.27, 05.19, 29.30, 05.20, 05.16, plus the decline rules from the meetings. EISA and FISA tabs. Built 9 Nov 2026 on top of L1–M.1.

### Done (9 Nov 2026)

- **Verification code (05.19):** `verificationCode.ts` `buildVerificationCode()`. EISA `QCTO/<last EISA date yyyymmdd>/V<yy>-<n>`, FISA `QCTOSP/<last FISA date yyyymmdd>/C<yy>-<nn>`. `<yy>` is the start year of QCTO's financial year (1 April to 31 March) of the decision date; `<n>` runs per financial year, separately for EISA and FISA. Set once in `finalDecision` (`ResultsFinalDecision`, `types.ts`) when the Chief Director approves or declines; shown on the batch, the Outcome Letter, the verification report and the "Sent to Certification" list.
- **Decline is final (29.27):** every QCTO stage's old "Decline" is now **Request more evidence** (reason required; the batch goes to the new stage `more-evidence`, "More evidence requested", and back to the QP/SDP, who resubmit as before). `evidenceRequestedAt` ("Date additional evidence requested") and `finalEvidenceReceivedAt` ("Date final outstanding evidence received") are recorded and shown. The Deputy Director and the Director can **Recommend decline** (reason required) while sending the batch on; it shows to the next role (`declineRecommendations`). Only the Chief Director can **Decline results**: it needs one or more irregularities and one or more corrective actions (`DeclineResultsForm`, enforced again in `api.ts` / `fisaApi.ts`), ends the batch as **Declined**, generates the decline Outcome Letter and notifies the QP/SDP. A declined batch has no resubmit action and no Post-EISA visit. `workflow.ts`: new action ids `*-request-evidence`, `dd-recommend-decline`, `director-recommend-decline`; `chief-director-decline` now goes to the final `declined` stage. A batch stored in the old `declined` stage with no `finalDecision` reads as `more-evidence` (`loadAll()` in both apis; idempotent).
- **Outcome Letter (29.30, 05.20):** `outcomeLetters.ts` builds QCTO's "Approval Letter – EISA" / "Decline Letter – EISA" and the "SP Approval Letter" (and the FISA decline version) as a model (`LetterModel`), shown by `OutcomeLetterView` and downloaded as HTML (`downloadLetterModel`, same approach as before, no new libraries). Tab and download are called **Outcome Letter** on every screen, never "Approval Letter". A DRAFT preview (marked "Draft", no code, signature pending) is available from the Deputy Director stage; the signed letter exists from the final decision, signed by the Chief Director who decided.
- **Certification recommendation letter (05.20):** `eisaRecommendationLetter()` / `fisaRecommendationLetter()`, approved batches only, internal only, addressed to the Director: Certification. Shown as a "Certification Recommendation" tab on the batch and in the "Documents for this batch" card.
- **Sent to Certification (internal only):** new tab in Approval of Results (`SentToCertificationTab.tsx`) listing every recommendation letter with batch, date sent, last assessment date, counts, verification code and a download. Audit entry reads "Recommendation letter sent to Certification" (EISA and FISA) instead of the old simulated text.
- **Downloads (05.16):** on every batch with a final decision the card gives the Outcome Letter (approval or decline), the verification report and, internally on approved batches only, the recommendation letter. The QP/SDP card is filtered with `documentsForPortal(..., 'external')` (recommendation letters are `internalOnly`).
- **Throughput rate:** EISA `wrote ÷ registered` (`BatchFigures.throughputRatePercent`) next to the competency rate and absenteeism on the batch figures, the internal and QP lists and the verification report; FISA `completed ÷ enrolled` (`fisaFiguresOf()`), on the FISA lists, batch detail and report.
- **FISA model:** learners can be **Absent** (`LearnerResultOutcome`); FISA competency rate is now competent ÷ completed (the same as before when nobody is absent). The batch carries `fisaDates` (the SDP enters the date of the FISA when creating the batch), `skillsProgrammeId`, `nqfLevel`, `credits`, and the ASD's `recommendationsForImprovement` (a field in the FISA compliance review, printed on the letter).
- **EISA model:** the batch carries `saqaId`, `nqfLevel`, `credits` (from the approved QASA) and `centres` (from the Confirmation).
- **Mock details:** `qpDirectory.ts` (code, contact title and surname, organisation, postal address), `sdpDirectory.ts` (accreditation number, contact, postal address), `skillsProgrammeDetails.ts`.

### Assumptions and open questions (Batch N1)

- **[ASSUMPTION]** The "FILE NAME" on the EISA recommendation letter is `QCTO03-<QP code>-<SAQA ID>-<last EISA date yyyymmdd>`. QCTO's real file-name pattern is unknown. One function: `eisaCertificationFileName()` in `config.ts`. The QP codes (`QP0101` …) are mock.
- **[ASSUMPTION]** "Director: Assessment" (signature line on both recommendation letters) is one config constant with a mock name, `DIRECTOR_ASSESSMENT_NAME` = "Dr Ntombi Sithebe", not a role in this build.
- **[ASSUMPTION]** The QP's contact person, organisation and postal address, the SDP's contact, address and accreditation number, and the Skills Programme ID/NQF/credits are mock details. The contact person of a QP is the QP themself (the directory holds one named person per QP). Real names or e-mail addresses are not used; the two fixed QCTO details you gave are used as written: the Certification address (256 Glyn Street, Hatfield, 0083) and the appeals mailbox in the EISA letters.
- **[ASSUMPTION]** The FISA verification number is padded to two digits (`C24-01`) and the EISA one is not (`V18-8`), as in QCTO's two examples.
- **[ASSUMPTION]** The financial year of a decision uses the date of the decision on this device (`toISOString`, so UTC), not South African local time. A decision between 00:00 and 02:00 SAST on 1 April is counted in the old year. One place to change if QCTO cares: `today()` in `api.ts` / `fisaApi.ts`.
- **[ASSUMPTION]** The FISA recommendation letter says "the attached <n> candidates" with n = the competent count, as on the EISA letter.
- **[ASSUMPTION — naming]** QCTO's FISA recommendation letter has a signature line "Assistant Director: Assessment". This build has no Assistant Director role (QCTO feedback 3.11), so the line reads "ASD: Assessment". One string in `outcomeLetters.ts`.
- **[ASSUMPTION]** The DRAFT preview shows the approval version (a decline needs the Chief Director's two lists). A decline recommended by the DD/Director is shown as a banner, not as a draft decline letter.
- **[ASSUMPTION]** The decline irregularities and corrective actions are typed one per line; blank lines are dropped.
- **[OPEN — QCTO]** The wording on the QP/SDP side when a batch is "More evidence requested" (the QP resubmits through the same Resubmit button).

### Seed (`SEED_VERSION = 'A16'`)

- Approved EISA batches (A8, A9, A10) and the approved FISA batch (R4) now carry verification codes, Outcome Letters and recommendation letters, and appear in "Sent to Certification". Numbers run in the order of the decisions inside financial year 2026/27.
- **A11** (Plumbing, was "Declined by the DD") is now **More evidence requested**.
- **A12** (new): Electrical Trades, EISA date today − 190, Thabo Technical College, 2 learners (one Not yet competent, one Absent). The DD and the Director recommended a decline; the Chief Director declined it with two irregularities and two corrective actions. Final, no Post-EISA visit. It needed a new past sitting, registration and confirmation, all added after the existing ones (calendar `s19`, registration P13, confirmation F14) so no earlier id moved.
- Every other stage, rate, mark and the Welding Post-EISA visit are unchanged.

**Checked:** 7-weekday seed check (Mon 2 to Sun 8 Nov 2026, 00:30 and 23:30 UTC, 14 runs, 126 checks each, all pass): EISA 11 batches (3 with the ASD, one each with the DD, Director and Chief Director, 3 approved, 1 More evidence requested, 1 Declined); Welding PEM visit unchanged (one, dateless, 40%) and none for the declined sitting; approved codes in the right format numbered 1 to 4 in decision order; approval letter subject, table, centre accreditation numbers, appeals wording and signer; decline letter without the competent/NYC/comply rows and with both lists; recommendation letter file name, table and three signature lines, and none for declined, more-evidence or in-progress batches; the QP's document list has no recommendation letter. Code numbering: a 31 March decision is `V26-n`, a 1 April decision restarts at `V27-1`, EISA and FISA numbered separately, examples `V18-8` and `C24-01` reproduced. Through the api: only the Chief Director can decline (Director and DD refused, empty lists refused), a recommend-decline needs a reason, shows at the Chief Director and moves the batch on, a declined batch cannot be resubmitted or approved and gets no Post-EISA visit and no recommendation letter, Request more evidence needs a reason and loops back (cycle 2, received date set), FISA does the same, an old `declined` batch reads as More evidence requested (twice), and "Recommendation letter sent to Certification" is in the audit trail with no "(simulated)" text left. `npx eslint src/features/assessments-v2` and the scoped `tsc -p` check pass clean.

## Batch N2 (QCTO change request doc v14 — Verification report, DD letter checklist, tracking registers, audit trail)

Rows 29.29, 29.28, 29.35, plus QCTO's templates. EISA and FISA tabs. Built 10 Nov 2026 on top of N1.

### Done (10 Nov 2026)

- **EISA Compliance Review = section 2 of QCTO's "EISA Quality Assurance Verification Report" (29.29):** `eisaReview.ts`, `mock/data/eisaVerificationLines.ts`, `components/EisaReviewForm.tsx`. 17 Yes/No lines, a comment required on every one, plus the free text (f summary and impact, k, m). Lines j, d, e and l are pre-filled from the system with the note "Pre-filled from the system" (`prefillEisaReview()`, run on submit and on resubmit). `assertEisaReviewComplete()` blocks the ASD's Send on. Earlier reviews stay read-only under "Previous checklist"; old-shape batches load with a blank review (idempotent migration in `loadAll`).
- **EISA verification report download (29.28):** `eisaVerificationReport.ts`, sections 1, 2, 4 and 5 in QCTO's layout; the A–F administration list is ticked "on file in the system" from the tagged documents (`eisaAdministrationRows()`); COMPILED / CHECKED / RECOMMENDED sign-off blocks; the DD letter checklist is included once completed.

The ASD fills E1 (system figures pre-filled), the DD fills E2 before sending on; Sections A–D name the SDP's tagged verification report. `assertFisaSectionEComplete()` blocks the ASD's Send on; resubmit clears Section E and keeps `firstSubmittedAt`.
- **DD "Approval of Results letter checklist" (EISA):** `letterChecklist.ts`, `components/DdLetterChecklistPanel.tsx`. Auto-filled table, tick lines, only the Deputy Director can tick, `assertDdChecklistComplete()` stops `dd-advance` and `dd-recommend-decline` until every required line is ticked; read-only afterwards with the DD's name and date, and included in the downloads. Cleared on resubmit.
- **Tracking registers (29.35):** `trackingRegister.ts`, `components/TrackingRegisterDownload.tsx`. EISA CSV with exactly 32 headings, FISA CSV with exactly 21; one row per approved batch, filtered on the approval-letter date; default range is the current QCTO quarter with quick picks for each quarter of the financial year. Working days use the shared helper (public holidays); quarter labels like `202526Q3`; dates `YYYY/MM/DD`; shared CSV writer (BOM and `sep=,`). Competency rate = competent ÷ wrote (EISA) or ÷ completed (FISA); throughput = wrote ÷ registered.
- **Audit trail CSV:** `components/AuditTrailCsvDownload.tsx`, per batch, internal only, with a date range.
- **File inputs:** buttons that click a hidden plain file input.

## Batch N.1 (Approval of Results — tab layout, documents on screen, N1/N2 test fixes)

Reorganised only: no feature, field, button or check was removed. `SEED_VERSION` is unchanged (A17): no seed change was needed.

### Bugs
- **A1 (FISA documents invisible to internal roles).** The seed and the code expose them: the approved Khanya FISA batch (R4) has a final decision with code `QCTOSP/20260929/C26-01`, an SP outcome letter, an SP certification recommendation letter and the ASD's recommendations text. No tab or box was gated on a role or on the ASD stage (the Outcome Letter and Certification Recommendation tabs were driven by `finalDecision` only). **I could not reproduce a gate in the code, so the cause is not proven.** The likeliest reasons they were missed: they sat behind a wrapping row of tabs, and the ASD's "Recommendations for improvements" box was one of seven blocks in the Compliance Review tab, greyed out once the batch moved on. Fix: the documents now have their own tabs, the recommendations box is on the Section E tab for every internal role (read-only past the ASD stage) and on the Summary of a decided batch, and the code is on the Summary. The seed check proves each of the four internal roles gets the code, outcome letter, recommendation letter, report and recommendations text on screen. If it is still seen in the browser, use "Reset demo data" first (stale data from an older seed would explain it) and note the role and tab.
- **A2 (leaks to the QP and SDP).** A real leak was found and fixed: the QP's and SDP's verification report contained the ASD's section 2 compliance review, the Deputy Director's letter checklist, the "E. QA Verification" lines (recommendation letter, checklist, tracking register), the ASD's bad/good practices and recommendation, the approval chain and the audit trail (with QCTO's comments). The external report is now a separate audience of the same model: no section 2, no E lines, no DD checklist, no ASD free text, annexures A and B only. The FISA SDP report has no Section E (outcome only). The QP/SDP tabs and Documents card never list the recommendation letter, checklist or tracking register.

### New files
- `documentModel.ts` (report/checklist model and HTML), `tabs.ts` (which tabs, pure), `nextStep.ts` (Next step card text), `components/DocumentView.tsx`, `Documents.tsx` (one component per document), `BatchTabs.tsx` (sideways-scrolling tab bar), `NextStepCard.tsx`, `ApprovalHistory.tsx`, `external/FisaSubmittedView.tsx`. `BatchDownloads.tsx` was removed (its two buttons are now the Download button on the Outcome Letter and Verification Report tabs, and the Documents card).
- The reports and the DD checklist are built as a model first; the screen and the download both read it. Letters already did (`outcomeLetters.ts`). Before the final decision the Outcome Letter, Verification Report and Letter Checklist carry a Draft banner on screen and in the download.

### Tab layout
Internal EISA: Summary, Learner Results, Evidence, Compliance Review, Letter Checklist, Approval Chain, Outcome Letter, Verification Report, Certification Recommendation, Audit Trail. Internal FISA: the same with Section E in place of Compliance Review and no Letter Checklist. QP/SDP: Summary, Learner Results, Evidence, Outcome Letter, Verification Report. Hidden when they do not apply: Letter Checklist and Outcome Letter before the Deputy Director stage; Certification Recommendation unless approved; the QP's and SDP's Outcome Letter and Verification Report until decided. There is no Progress tab: this module has no progress view for the QP or SDP (the request said "if the module has it").

Details-tab mapping (EISA):

| Was on Details | Now on |
|---|---|
| Status badge | Summary |
| Final decision notice, evidence dates | Summary |
| QP, Qualification, EISA date, Submission | Summary |
| Batch figures | Summary (as stat tiles) |
| Post-EISA banner | Summary |
| Learner results table and learner detail | Learner Results |
| Marking & Moderation monitoring status | Compliance Review |
| Evidence documents, Already on file, Declaration | Evidence |
| Documents for this batch card | Evidence |

Also moved: the ASD's action bar (was at the bottom of Compliance Review) and the DD/Director/CD bar (Approval Chain) are now ONE bar at the bottom of Approval Chain; the Download buttons that sat in Approval Chain are on the document tabs; the DD letter checklist moved from the Outcome Letter tab to Letter Checklist; the duplicate evidence lists inside Compliance Review are shown once, on Evidence ("Add an evidence file" stays in Compliance Review).

Details-tab mapping (FISA):

| Was on Details | Now on |
|---|---|
| Status, final decision notice, evidence dates | Summary |
| SDP, Skills Programme, Date of FISA, Submitted | Summary |
| Competency and throughput rate | Summary (stat tiles) |
| Declaration | Evidence |
| Learner list | Learner Results |
| Evidence list, Documents for this batch | Evidence |

The old FISA Compliance Review tab (notes, evidence notes, recommendations, E1, folder checklist) and the E2 form that sat in Approval Chain are now all on **Section E** (E2 shows a short note until the batch reaches the Deputy Director).

Buttons: secondary actions (Request more evidence, Recommend decline, Decline) are on the left and the primary action on the right, in the same order on every stage (`ResultsActionBar.tsx`).

### SDP signposting
A card at the top of the FISA module (SDP) with "Go to FISA results"; the matching card on Approval of Results → FISA with a button back; one-line descriptions on the SDP dashboard rows for FISA and Approval of Results. Wording is in `config/labels.ts`.

### Assumptions
- The QP's EISA report omits the ASD's recommendation, bad practices and good practices from section 5 (they come from the ASD's section 2 review).
- The Letter Checklist tab shows the interactive checklist to the Deputy Director at the DD stage and the read-only document to everyone else.
- "Generated on" was dropped from the report header so the screen and the download are identical.

### Checked
Scratchpad check (`n1check.tsx`, bundled with esbuild and rendered with react-dom/server for every seeded EISA and FISA batch): 1,220 checks pass, 0 fail. Approved FISA batch: all four internal roles get the code, outcome letter, SP recommendation letter, report (title, Section E, A–D line) and recommendations text on screen; no Draft banner on final documents. Every document tab renders for approved, declined and in-progress batches (EISA and FISA, all four internal roles, QP and SDP). QP/SDP tabs never contain the compliance review, E1/E2, the letter checklist, the tracking register, the recommendation letter or annexures C/D. The Draft banner is present exactly when the batch is undecided. `eslint src/features/assessments-v2` and the scoped `tsc -p` check pass clean.

## Batch P (QCTO change request doc v14, rows 05.18 and 05.21 — FISA results corrections)

FISA tab of Approval of Results only; EISA unchanged. `SEED_VERSION = 'A18'`.

### Done (10 Nov 2026)
- **05.18 FISA evidence tags** (`config.ts`, `types.ts`). Learner spreadsheet, Verification report, Assessment instrument, Attendance register, Completed practical rubric, Other evidence, each with QCTO's hint under the picker. "Marksheet" is now Learner spreadsheet and "Moderation report" is now Verification report. Required before Submit: one Learner spreadsheet, one Verification report and one Assessment instrument; the blocking list says which is missing (`workflow.ts` `fisaSubmissionIssues`). Learner spreadsheet and Verification report are single-use (they leave the picker once used); a file's tag is fixed once added (a static label, no dropdown), and the api refuses a changed tag, a repeated single-use tag and any tag that is not on the list (`fisaApi.ts` `assertEvidenceRules`).
- **Stored tags mapped on load** (`fisaApi.ts` `loadAll`): marksheet becomes learner-spreadsheet and moderation-report becomes verification-report. A new tag maps to itself, so it can run any number of times.
- **05.21 only enrolled learners, no calculation.** The draft lists the learners the SDP has enrolled for this batch's skills programme (`mock/data/skillsProgrammeEnrolments.ts`, `qaDomain.listEnrolledLearnersForSkillsProgramme`); there is no way to type a learner in. The api refuses any other learner with "This learner is not enrolled with your SDP for this skills programme." (save and submit), takes the name and ID number from the enrolment record, and refuses any outcome that is not Competent, Not yet competent or Absent.
- **Learner spreadsheet = QCTO sheet 3 "Learner Data & Achievement"** (`fisaLearnerSpreadsheet.ts`, `external/FisaSpreadsheetUpload.tsx`). Download has exactly the 40 headings in QCTO's order, one row per learner on the batch, BOM and `sep=,` from the shared writer; SDP Accreditation Number, National ID, last/first name, SkillsProgramme ID and LearnerEnrolledDate (YYYYMMDD) are pre-filled, DATE OF FISA, FINAL FISA RESULT and Date Submitted to QCTO are blank, the rest blank. Upload uses the shared parser (both delimiters, `sep=` line, BOM, leading 0 restored), matches headings case-insensitively, ignores other columns and previews an error per row: not enrolled, on the batch but missing from the file, result not 555/999/777 (message lists all three), DATE OF FISA not YYYYMMDD or not the batch's FISA date, SkillsProgramme ID or SDP Accreditation Number not matching, invalid SA ID number, learner twice. A clean file fills the outcomes (555 Competent, 999 Not yet competent, 777 Absent), which the SDP can still edit, and is attached as the Learner spreadsheet evidence in the same save.
- **Codes shown** as "Competent (555)", "Not yet competent (999)", "Absent (777)" on the FISA learner list (screen and draft dropdown), the FISA verification report's learner table, and next to the counts on the FISA outcome letter ("No. declared Competent (C), code 555"). The counts themselves are unchanged.

## Batch Q1 (QCTO 5 Nov 33:51–36:17 row 05.10, and 29 Oct 1:22:27–1:27:41 row 29.13 — EISA Calendar)

EISA Calendar only. `SEED_VERSION = 'A19'`. The normal (not on-demand) date and registration rules are untouched.

### Done (10 Nov 2026)
- **05.10 Recommend / Do not recommend** (`workflow.ts`, `changeRequests.ts`, `types.ts`). At every recommendation stage (DD on a postponement; DD and then Director on a cancellation) there are two actions, `request-recommend` and the new `request-not-recommend`. Both need a comment (`requiresNote`), both go to the same next stage as before, and `requiresDifferentActor` and the "nobody acts twice on one request" rule (`hasActedOnRequest`) are unchanged.
- **Stored on the request** (`recommendations[]`: person, role, `recommends`, comment, date; older records without `recommends` read as "Recommend"). `ChangeRequestDetailDialog.tsx` shows a "Recommendations" list above the comment box and the Approve / Decline buttons, and in the History, internal portal only. The QP's detail has neither the list nor the History.
- **Decider.** Director (postponement) and CEO (cancellation) can approve or decline whatever was recommended. If they approve a request that has a "Do not recommend", the request stores `decidedAgainst` (the roles), the decision shows "Decided against the recommendation of DD" (neutral line, no dialog) and an audit entry with the same text is written. Declining writes nothing extra.
- **Status labels.** Internal: "With the CEO for decision — DD: Recommend, Director: Do not recommend" (`changeRequestInternalStatusLabel`). QP side: the stage only.
- **Notifications** (role level). Raised: DD and Director both told (the QP as before). Recommended / not recommended: the next role is told which, the QP is told only the new stage (the old "was recommended" wording is gone, because it would give the answer away). Decided: DD, Director and QP told.
- **29.13 On-demand sittings** (`config.ts`, `dateChecks.ts`, `registrationClosing.ts`, `shared/dates/workingDays.ts`). A qualification whose approved QASA says `eisaSittingsOnDemand` needs a date at least `ON_DEMAND_MIN_NOTICE_MONTHS = 1` calendar month after today instead of the 21-working-day lead time (weekends and holidays still refused), and its registration closes `ON_DEMAND_REGISTRATION_CLOSE_WORKING_DAYS = 5` working days before the EISA date (open until midnight of that day). `checkSittingDate`, `calcRegistrationClosingDate` and `isRegistrationClosed` take an optional `onDemand` flag; the flag lives on the sitting (`EisaSitting.onDemand`, set from the batch, or looked up when the ASD adds or edits a sitting), so every screen that shows or uses a closing date (calendar list, month, details, QP and ASD batch views, registration list and its "closed" check) follows it.
- **Hint line** "On-demand (trade test): at least one month's notice. QCTO is trying this as a pilot." (`config/labels.ts` `eisaOnDemandHint`) on the propose form and on on-demand rows of the bulk-upload preview.
- **Schedule change notice.** When a QP proposes, bulk-uploads, resubmits or edits dates for an on-demand qualification, the calendar reviewer (ASD) and the DD are told "<QP> updated the on-demand schedule for <qualification> (<n> dates)" (`api.ts` `notifyOnDemandScheduleChange`).

### Assumptions and open questions (Batch Q1)
- **ASSUMPTION for QCTO to confirm: one month.** QCTO suggested trying one calendar month as a pilot and still has to confirm it (`ON_DEMAND_MIN_NOTICE_MONTHS`).
- **ASSUMPTION for QCTO to confirm: five working days.** Registration for an on-demand sitting closes five working days before the EISA date (`ON_DEMAND_REGISTRATION_CLOSE_WORKING_DAYS`). Nobody at QCTO has given this figure.
- **Open: is one month really "shorter"?** The normal rule is `MIN_LEAD_TIME_WORKING_DAYS = 21` working days, which is about 4 weeks plus any holidays, so a calendar month is about the same length (a little longer in most months). If QCTO means a clearly shorter notice for trade tests, the constant needs to be smaller (for example two weeks) or the normal lead time reconsidered.
- "Decided against the recommendation" is recorded only when a "Do not recommend" is approved. A decline that goes against a "Recommend" is not flagged (not asked for).
- The postponement's proposed new date for an on-demand sitting uses the on-demand notice rule.
- A request raised before this batch has no `recommends` on its recommendations and reads as "Recommend".

### Seed (`SEED_VERSION = 'A19'`)
Every existing record keeps its stage and its id (new sittings and requests are added last). Added:
- **R4**: cancellation of S5 (Welding, Precious Mahlangu) at the CEO. DD (Grace) "Recommend", Director (Zanele) "Do not recommend", both with comments.
- **R5**: postponement of S4 (Electrical Trades, Nomvula Dlamini) at the Director, with a DD (Peter) "Do not recommend" and a comment.
- **B4**: an on-demand proposal for Gas Practitioner (Johan van der Merwe), one date about six weeks out, waiting for the ASD, with the two schedule-change notifications. Its registration closing date is five working days before the date.
- **`mock/data/accreditedQualifications.ts`**: Gas Practitioner (SAQA 101941, Johan, Lerato Training Institute) added. It had no accredited SDP, so it could not appear on the calendar at all. Side effects: it now appears in the QP's propose list and in the RPL/ARPL qualification picker.
- Notifications: every seeded request now also has the Director's "raised" notice, and R1's decision the DD and Director notices; the QP's "recommended" notices read "is now <stage>".

### Checked
7-weekday seed check (scratchpad `check.ts`, bundled with esbuild, fake dates Mon 9 to Sun 15 Nov 2026 at 00:30 and 23:30 UTC, 14 runs, 54 checks each, all pass): 5 requests, R1 to R3 at the same stages, R4 and R5 as described, labels (internal has the recommendations, QP has the stage only), no QP notification carries a recommendation or comment; 4 batches and 27 sittings, B4 on-demand at ASD review with its date 42 to 46 days out, its closing date equal to an independent count of five working days back (closing day open, next day closed); the on-demand check (one month passes, just inside fails, weekends refused) and the normal rule unchanged (21 working days passes, 20 fails, 15 Jun 2027 closes 15 Mar 2027); a proposal inside one month refused, an on-demand proposal notifies the ASD and DD with the exact text, an edit notifies again, a non-on-demand proposal sends nothing; the bulk-upload on-demand row uses the on-demand closing date and a date inside a month is flagged. Through the api: recommend and do-not-recommend refused without a comment, the DD cannot act twice, a person who recommended cannot decide, decline needs a comment, a CEO approving against the DD is recorded (`decidedAgainst`, audit entry, sitting cancelled), DD, Director and QP told of the decision, declining against a "Do not recommend" records nothing, R5 approved by the Director against the DD (sitting postponed). `npx eslint src/features/assessments-v2` and the scoped `tsc -p` check pass clean. Not yet clicked through in a browser.

## Batch R1 (QCTO 29 Oct 2:05:33, 2:14:11 to 2:16:29; 6 Aug 2:03:42 to 2:06:39; process map "EISA Registration" — Learner EISA Registration)

Learner EISA Registration only (`modules/eisa-registration/`, plus the mock QA domain). `SEED_VERSION = 'A20'`. The existing CR-041 compliance gate (`startRegistration`) is untouched.

### Done (10 Nov 2026)
- **29.16 formal and RPL counts** (`summary.ts` `learnerCountText`). "<n> learners: <f> formal, <r> RPL/ARPL" (singular "1 learner") on the ASD list and workspace, the SDP list and detail, and the notification sent on submission (`api.ts`; resubmission after a return says "EISA registration resubmitted"). Declined learners are not in `learners`, so they are never counted.
- **29.18 SDP monitoring status** (`config.ts` `MONITORING_VALID_YEARS = 2`, `monitoring.ts`, `mock/data/sdpMonitoring.ts`, `qaDomain.getSdpLastMonitoringDate`, `components/MonitoringStatusLine.tsx`). One date per SDP (per institution). "Monitored on <date>, valid until <date + 2 years>", "Monitoring expired on <valid-until date>" or "Never monitored", on the ASD workspace and the SDP detail. Expired or never monitored: `reg-create-number` is refused in the api with the reason, and on screen the button is disabled with a red message. The SDP can still build and submit (a line on their detail says so). The last monitoring date is held as "days before today" so the demo statuses never change as the calendar moves.
- **Return to SDP** (`workflow.ts` `reg-return-to-sdp`, `ad-review` to `sdp-submission`, reason required; `api.ts` `performRegistrationAction`). Stored in `record.returns` (cycle, reason, who, when). The SDP gets a notification with the reason and cycle, sees it on the detail (amber box) and can change learners or SORs and submit again. The status reads "Returned to SDP (cycle n)" while it is back with the SDP. Learners, SORs and audit entries are kept; the audit entry is "Return to SDP" with the reason as its note.
- **Decline learner** (`api.ts` `declineLearner`, reason required, only while with the ASD). The learner moves to `record.declinedLearners` (reason, date, who) and shows in a "Declined learners" list on both screens. The SDP is told "<learner> will not be registered for <qualification> (EISA <date>): <reason>"; audit entry. A declined learner no longer blocks "Create registration number" (only still-flagged learners do). A registration with no learner left cannot get a number: "Every learner on this registration has been declined…" on screen and in the api. A learner declined from this registration cannot be added to it again.
- **Duplicate-registration block** (`api.ts` `findConcurrentRegistration`, now returns `{ registration, reason }`). A learner is blocked while in another registration that is still open, or registered for a sitting whose EISA results batch containing them is not yet approved. Once that batch is approved (competent or not) the block lifts. A registration they were declined from and a cancelled sitting never block. Message wording kept, reason added: "…cannot register for another at the same time until the results for that sitting are approved." (for an open registration: "until that registration is complete and the results for that sitting are approved").
- Stored records without `declinedLearners` / `returns` read as having none (`loadAll`), nothing written back.

### Assumptions and open questions (Batch R1)
- **Open (QCTO 29 Oct 2:06:37): who checks registrations?** The ASD does, as today (`REGISTRATION_PROCESSOR_ROLE`). QCTO mentioned an intern or administrator; if that is a separate role, it changes `config.ts` and the registration workflow's `ad-review` stage only.
- **ASSUMPTION: two years** of monitoring validity (`MONITORING_VALID_YEARS`). "Valid until" is the same day two years on (29 Feb clamps to 28 Feb); the last day itself is still valid.
- **ASSUMPTION: "Monitoring expired on" shows the day it expired** (last monitoring plus two years), not the last monitoring date.
- **Open: a results batch that was declined (final).** The rule says the block lifts when the batch is approved, so a learner on a finally declined batch stays blocked. Probably wrong in practice (nothing more can happen to that batch); QCTO to say whether a final decline should lift it too (one condition in `findConcurrentRegistration`).
- A registered learner who is not on the sitting's results batch at all (never reached confirmation) stays blocked, because no approved batch contains them.
- "Return to SDP" keeps every SOR status (accepted, flagged and so on) as it was; the SDP decides what to change. Declining is not undone by a return.
- Monitoring of Vuma Trade Centre (non-compliant) is seeded valid; it has no registration, so it shows nowhere.

- `mock/data/sdpMonitoring.ts`: Khanya (200 days ago) and Vuma (400) valid, **Lerato expired** (900 days ago; its registration G7, with the ASD, shows the block), **Thabo never monitored**.
- **G8** (new, last): Thabo, Electrical Trades (second date of the open batch), with the ASD, learners **learner-005 (Lindiwe Zulu)** and **learner-052 (Onalenna Modise)**, who are in the registration behind the approved Electrical Trades results (today - 170) as Not yet competent, so they are registered again.
